# Inline icon image audit

Survey of every `<img>` tag using the inline-icon style pattern (`style={{... display: 'inline' ...}}`) — the signature of an icon image that has not yet been migrated to the Domo icon font (`icon-*` / `legacy-icon-*`).

Generated by scanning every `*.mdx` file in the repository.

---

## Summary

| Metric | Count |
|---|---:|
| Total inline icon-image references | **3,672** |
| Distinct image `src` paths | **2,870** |
| Articles containing at least one inline icon image | **722** |

|---|---:|---:|

| `ja` | 1,600 | 1,782 |
| `de` | 18 | 41 |
| `es` | 19 | 39 |
| `kb` | 23 | 23 |
